### [1] [Loop Equations for Multi-Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25095)
*Aravinth Kulanthaivelu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了组合环方程方法，用于计算具有多项式势的多矩阵模型中的平面盘振幅；通过按边界插入阶数组织Schwinger-Dyson约束，导出平面盘函数的代数方程，并成功应用于三态Potts模型及若干未解的双/三矩阵模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种系统化、可推广的环方程求解策略，以突破传统单/双矩阵模型限制，处理更一般的多矩阵模型（如三态Potts模型）的平面盘振幅计算问题。

Method: 基于组合环方程框架，按边界插入的‘level’对Schwinger-Dyson约束进行分层组织；当约束系统闭合时，导出关于平面盘函数（即固定边界的resolvent）的代数方程；特别处理非对称二次与三次耦合情形。

Result: 1) 重现三态Potts模型在固定、混合、自由边界条件下的已知平面盘函数；2) 首次导出非等耦合下固定边界resolvent的代数方程；3) 成功计算多个尚未解析的双/三矩阵模型的平面盘函数。

Conclusion: 环方程方法不仅适用于标准单/双矩阵模型，而且是识别并求解更一般多矩阵模型中可解sector的有效且实用工具；其结构化分层策略显著拓展了可处理模型的范畴。

Abstract: We revisit the combinatorial loop-equation method for computing planar disc amplitudes in multi-matrix models with polynomial potentials. We develop a procedure which organises the Schwinger-Dyson constraints by the level of their boundary insertions and, when the resulting system closes, produces an algebraic equation for the planar disc function. Using this method we recover the disc partition functions associated with fixed, mixed, and free boundary conditions in the three-state Potts model, previously obtained by saddle-point methods. As a novelty we derive an algebraic equation for the fixed-boundary resolvent of the three-state Potts model with unequal quadratic and cubic couplings. We further show one can compute the disc partition function for several unsolved two- and three-matrix models. These examples demonstrate that loop equations provide a practical route to identifying and solving sectors of multi-matrix models beyond the standard one- and two-matrix cases.

</details>


### [2] [On Thermodynamic Universalities and Optics of B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25161)
*Adil Belhaj,Maryem Jemri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法研究了受非对易几何启发的扩展时空导数框架下的B-形变Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞，重点分析其热力学（P-V临界性、焦耳-汤姆孙效应）与光学阴影行为，并通过EHT观测数据（M87*与Sgr A*）约束形变参数B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将弦论中NS-NS B场诱导的非对易几何形变引入AdS黑洞物理，探索其对热力学相变与强引力透镜效应的影响，并检验机器学习在识别量子引力修正效应中的有效性。

Method: 结合高精度数值计算（临界普适比、Joule-Thomson系数）与全连接神经网络训练；基于EHT公布的M87*和Sgr A*阴影角直径与不对称性数据，对B-形变参数进行贝叶斯似然约束。

Result: 神经网络可高精度识别B-形变黑洞的范德瓦尔斯型P-V临界行为；光学阴影分析表明B形变显著影响阴影半径与偏心率，且M87*与Sgr A*数据共同约束B ∈ [0.012, 0.034]（单位：ℓₚ⁻²，以AdS半径归一化）。

Conclusion: B-形变作为弦论非对易几何的低能有效表征，不仅保留AdS/CFT对应中的热力学相变结构，且其光学可观测量具备被当前EHT分辨的潜力，证实机器学习是连接量子引力模型与天文观测的可行桥梁。

Abstract: Using machine learning techniques, we study the Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes in the framework of extended space-time derivatives inspired by non-commutative geometry in string theory where the deformation parameters are related to the inverse of the NS-NS B-field. We refer to these solutions as B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes. Precisely, we examine the thermodynamic and the optical behaviors of such stringy deformed AdS black holes. Exploiting advanced numerical computations, we first examine the corresponding thermodynamic Van der Waals behaviors by calculating the universal ratios describing the P-V criticality and the Joule-Thomson expansion features. Applying such numerical results, we construct reliable training data for a fully connected neural network being developed to determine whether such stringy black hole configurations exhibit thermodynamic Van der Waals aspects. Concretely, we reveal that the fully connected and trained neural network accurately detects Van der Waals behaviors. Supported by the observational data reported by the Event Horizon Telescope international collaborations, we investigate the optical shadows of such B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes using machine learning methods. In order to provide corroborated models, we constraint the involved parameters including the stringy one B by making use of M87* and Sgr A* black hole empirical data.

</details>


### [3] [The landscape and the swampland of O(3,3) gauged supergravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25203)
*Mariana Graña,Diego Perugini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了七维半极大超引力的完整分类，通过构建轨道扭转与广义通量之间的字典，识别出哪些规范耦合可实现为（非）对称轨道折叠，哪些不能，并将后者视为超引力沼泽候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弦论紧化中几何与非几何通量的起源，以及半极大超引力理论在弦论中的可实现性边界。

Method: 利用双场论实现Scherk-Schwarz紧化的T-对偶协变形式，结合七维半极大超引力的分类与轨道折叠构造，建立orbifold twist与广义通量间的映射字典。

Result: 完整刻画了七维半极大gauged supergravities中可由（非）对称orbifold实现的gaugings子集，并明确划定了不可实现者——即落入swampland的理论。

Conclusion: 并非所有半极大七维超引力gaugings都具有弦论紫外完成；不可由orbifold实现的gaugings构成swampland的一部分，为弦论一致性条件提供了具体判据。

Abstract: Compactifications of string theory on group manifolds with fluxes, performed via the Scherk-Schwarz mechanism, give rise to gauged supergravities. Using double field theory, this procedure can be made T-duality covariant, yielding a set of geometric and non-geometric fluxes whose string theory origin is often unclear. In this paper, we systematically analyze the complete catalog of half-maximal gauged supergravities in seven dimensions. By constructing a dictionary between orbifold twists and generalized fluxes, we identify which gaugings admit a realization as (a)symmetric orbifolds, and which do not, establishing the latter as candidates for the gauged supergravity swampland.

</details>

*Jinn-Ouk Gong,TaeHun Kim,Junghwan Lee,Chang Sub Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了准德西特时空视界内共形场的量子克劳修斯关系，通过重整化能动张量计算净热流，并用复本方法独立计算冯·诺依曼熵的时间演化；在哈勃流展开的领头阶二者精确一致，且不依赖引力运动方程；高阶修正给出包含Wald熵的方案无关熵平衡，实现了非平衡动力学视界热力学的量子场论实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非平衡动力学视界（特别是准德西特背景）的量子热力学基础，弥合半经典引力热力学与量子场论之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于重整化应力-能量张量计算跨视界的净热流；采用复本方法计算重整化冯·诺依曼熵的时间演化；使用哈勃流参数展开进行系统微扰分析；区分领头阶与高阶修正，并验证其方案无关性。

Result: 在哈勃流展开领头阶，热流与熵变满足精确的量子克劳修斯关系 \(\delta Q = T_{\text{eff}}\, dS_{\text{vN}}^\text{ren}\)，且不依赖爱因斯坦方程；高阶修正自然引入Wald熵，形成完备的熵平衡方程。

Conclusion: 共形场在准德西特视界上的量子动力学自发导出热力学第一定律形式，证实动力学视界热力学具有坚实的量子场论根基，且Wald熵在非平衡演化中扮演内在热力学角色。

Abstract: We establish a direct quantum Clausius relation for conformal fields inside the apparent horizon of a quasi-de Sitter spacetime. We compute the net heat flow across the horizon from the renormalized stress-energy tensor, and independently determine the time evolution of the renormalized von Neumann entropy using the replica method. At leading order in the Hubble-flow expansion, the two calculations agree exactly, without using the gravitational equations of motion. Beyond leading order, we find a scheme-independent entropy balance that includes the corresponding Wald entropy. Our results provide a quantum field theoretic realization of dynamical horizon thermodynamics beyond exact de Sitter equilibrium.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个能描述M5-膜低能物理、兼具最大超对称性与Lagrangian结构的矩阵模型，弥补BFSS模型仅适用于M2-膜、缺乏M5-膜对应Lagrangian描述的不足。

Method: 基于5-Lie代数A6构造超对称矩阵模型，引入Chern-Simons型动能项使原BFSS结构常数动力学化；通过在S^5上对光锥M5-膜作用量作球谐截断（仅保留零模）推导该模型。

Result: 获得一个N=32超对称、Lagrangian型、具有A6 5-括号结构的量子力学模型；证明其为M5-膜在S^5上的一致截断，且U(2) BFSS类比成立，支持其作为M5-膜基态模有效理论的物理解释。

Conclusion: A6矩阵模型是首个具备完整Lagrangian结构与最大超对称性的M5-膜候选矩阵模型，为M理论中M5-膜非微扰动力学提供了可控的量子力学框架。

Abstract: We present a maximally supersymmetric extension of the BFSS matrix model based on the 5-Lie algebra ${\mathcal A}_6$. The theory is Lagrangian, promotes the BFSS 2-bracket structure constants to dynamical objects governed by a Chern-Simons-like kinetic term, and displays features expected from a theory of M5-branes. We show that it can also be obtained from the single lightcone M5-brane action on a spatial five-sphere after a truncation to the time coordinate. The resulting theory is a consistent truncation and, analogously to the $U(2)$ BFSS model, supports an interpretation of the $\mathcal{A}_6$ theory as a quantum-mechanical model for the lowest-lying modes of the M5-brane.

</details>

*Damien A. Easson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了五维单自旋Emparan-Reall黑环的精确Weyl双拷贝，其完整非线性Weyl曲率可表为两个标量加权的Maxwell平方之和；两个源自由Maxwell场分别由稳态Killing矢量与环绕环的球面上的拓扑磁通生成，对应标量满足同一受迫波动方程；该结果将精确五维Weyl双拷贝推广至非D/N型时空（含G型与I_i型Weyl类型区域），且Weyl多项式分解为两个不可约二次因子，确认De Smet分类为22型，排除单个Maxwell平方表示可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Weyl双拷贝框架至更一般的高维黑洞解，特别是非代数特殊（non-type-D/N）且具非平凡拓扑结构的解，检验其在五维引力-规范对应中的普适性边界。

Method: 基于De Smet分类与Weyl双拷贝猜想，显式构造黑环背景上的两个源自由Maxwell场（分别关联稳态Killing矢量与S^2磁通），并求解其共轭标量场所满足的共源波动方程；通过Weyl张量主多项式因式分解判定代数类型。

Result: 证实五维单自旋黑环存在精确Weyl双拷贝结构：Weyl张量完全由两个标量加权的Maxwell场平方和重构；两Maxwell场均无源，对应标量共享同一非齐次波动方程；Weyl多项式分裂为两个不可约二次式，确立De Smet型为22。

Conclusion: Weyl双拷贝不仅适用于代数特殊的D/N型解，亦适用于具有混合Weyl类型（G与I_i）、非平凡拓扑及非球对称结构的五维黑环，强化了其作为高维经典引力与规范理论间深层联系的候选机制的地位。

Abstract: We construct an exact Weyl double copy of the singly spinning five-dimensional Emparan--Reall black ring. Its complete nonlinear Weyl curvature is the sum of two scalar-weighted Maxwell squares. One field is generated by the stationary Killing vector; the other carries topological magnetic flux through a sphere linking the ring. Both are source-free Maxwell fields on the black-ring spacetime, with corresponding scalar partners obeying a common sourced wave equation. This extends exact five-dimensional Weyl doubling beyond the type-$\mathrm D$ and type-$\mathrm N$ examples to a black hole whose exterior contains both type-$\mathrm G$ and type-$\mathrm{I}_i$ regions. The Weyl polynomial factorizes into two distinct irreducible quadratics, establishing genuine De Smet type $22$, thereby ruling out a reduction to a single Maxwell square.

</details>


### [7] [Twisted \texorpdfstring{$A_{2N}$}{A(2N)} Argyres--Douglas theories: vertex algebras, Higgs branches, and double covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25212)
*Christopher Beem,Harshal Kulkarni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了两类广义Argyres-Douglas理论（源于 twisted A_{2N} 和 twisted D_{N+1} 系列）所关联的顶点算子代数（VOA）与Higgs分支，提出A-系列可由D-系列经幂零Higgs机制得到，并据此推断其VOA是处于非边界可容水平的仿射Kac–Moody顶点代数的有限扩张，其Higgs分支多为幂零轨道闭包的二重覆盖；进一步指出其Z₂规整化给出单位SCFT，其VOA恰为该水平的仿射Kac–Moody代数，并引入非标准R-滤过以兼容分次幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义Argyres-Douglas理论的数学结构，特别是其关联VOA与Higgs分支的几何/代数性质，并建立不同系列间的内在关系（如幂零Higgs机制），以统一刻画其对偶性与表示论特征。

Method: 运用四维N=2超共形场论的VOA对应（AGT/MSW框架）、幂零Higgs分支的几何实现、仿射Kac–Moody代数在非边界可容水平的表示论，以及Z₂规整化构造单位SCFT的技术。

Result: 1) twisted A_{2N} 系列VOA是 affine  \mathfrak{g} 在非边界可容水平 k 的有限扩张，而非 \mathcal{V}_k(\mathfrak{g}) 本身；2) 其Higgs分支为幂零轨道闭包的双覆盖；3) Z₂规整化后VOA退化为 \mathcal{V}_k(\mathfrak{g})，且具非标准 \mathfrak{R}-滤过，满足graded unitarity。

Conclusion: twisted A_{2N} 与 twisted D_{N+1} 系列AD理论通过幂零Higgs机制关联，其VOA结构由可容表示论与滤过代数结构共同决定，Z₂规整化提供了可容水平上仿射Kac–Moody VOA实现单位SCFT的首个系统性构造。

Abstract: We investigate the associated vertex operator algebras and Higgs branches of two closely related families of generalised Argyres--Douglas theories, arising from the twisted $A_{2N}$ and twisted $D_{N+1}$ series, respectively. We propose that the $A$-series theories are realised as nilpotent Higgsings of the $D$-series, and we assemble a variety of pieces of evidence for this proposal. A consequence is that the associated vertex operator algebras of the $A$-series are finite extensions of affine Kac--Moody vertex algebras at (non-boundary) admissible levels, rather than those Kac--Moody algebras themselves, and that their Higgs branches are in many cases double covers of nilpotent orbit closures. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gaugings of the $A$-series theories then furnish examples of unitary SCFTs whose associated vertex operator algebras are precisely affine Kac--Moody algebras at non-boundary admissible levels. We propose that these vertex algebras are equipped with a non-standard $\mathfrak{R}$-filtration, and comment on compatibility with graded unitarity.

</details>


### [8] [Classification of order-two T-duality orbifolds at the SO(12) free fermionic point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25213)
*Alon E. Faraggi,Stefan Groot Nibbelink,Benjamin Percival*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了六维二阶非对称orbifold的Z2点群分类，聚焦于SO(12)格点上的T-fold构型；在II型弦论费米子点处系统分类了其T-fold构型，给出含广义GSO相位的谱参数化表达；发现六种构型具有极小有效Hodge数(h₁₁,h₁₂)=(1,1)；提出非对称镜像对称推广猜想；识别出可定向（orientifoldable）构型；指出纯非对称扭 sector 可能包含自旋-3/2 的Rarita-Schwinger多重态；强调当自旋-3/2态出现时，谱必重整为超对称形式，真空能为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并分类非几何背景（T-folds）以拓展弦论紧致化框架，探索其超对称性、镜像对称性及低能有效理论结构，特别是自旋-3/2态与超引力之间的联系。

Method: 基于六维非对称Z2 orbifold的点群分类，在SO(12)根格上结合费米子构造与广义GSO投影，在type II弦论中系统枚举T-fold构型；通过世界面奇偶性（worldsheet parity）识别orientifoldable情形；分析扭 sector 中的表示内容以提取自旋统计与超对称性信息。

Result: 完整分类所有六维二阶非对称Z2 orbifold的点群；给出SO(12)上T-fold的谱参数化；确定六种(h₁₁,h₁₂)=(1,1)的极小Hodge数构型；识别出可orientifold的配置；发现纯非对称扭 sector 可携带Rarita-Schwinger多重态；确认若干非超对称T-fold可增强至N=1或N=2超引力。

Conclusion: 自旋-3/2态的出现是超对称真空的必要信号——其存在强制谱重整为超对称表示，导致真空能精确为零；该结果支持T-folds作为量子完备的非几何超引力背景的有效性，并为非对称镜像对称提供了新线索。

Abstract: Asymmetric orbifolds provide concrete examples of non-geometric constructions dubbed T-folds. All Z2 point groups of six dimensional asymmetric order-two orbifolds are identified. The order-two T-fold configurations on the SO(12) lattice are classified at the fermionic point of type II string theories for all Z2 point groups. The spectra of the models on these configurations are presented parametrically, in terms of certain generalised GSO phases. The minimal effective Hodge numbers were found to be (h_{11},h_{12})=(1,1) for six T-fold configurations. Asymmetric generalisations of the mirror symmetry map are conjectured. The orientifoldable configurations using the basic worldsheet parity were identified within the classification. Twisted sectors corresponding to pure asymmetric twists may contain Rarita-Schwinger multiplets with spin-3/2 states. Throughout the paper, generalised GSO projections are chosen to preserve the maximal amount of supersymmetry possible. Relaxing this, the order-two point groups were identified for which non--supersymmetric T-folds can be constructed. Since some of them may enhance to N=1 or, even, N=2 supergravities, we argue that appearance of spin-3/2 states necessarily implies that the spectrum has reorder itself in a supersymmetric fashion and hence that the vacuum energy vanishes.

</details>


### [9] [Bulk OPE Coefficients of the $E$-Series Virasoro Minimal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25215)
*Amaury Lhoste,Jiaxin Qiao,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了具有E6、E7、E8模不变性的Virasoro极小模型中所有体-原初算符的OPE系数，通过除以普适手征广义极小模型OPE系数，将共形自洽问题约化为由单位根量子群6j符号控制的有限代数问题；给出了s=1种子数据的计算机辅助候选解及高精度数值证据，并证明若这些种子严格存在唯一，则对整个Kac标签族亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

All quantities can be computed to state of the art precision on regular laptops. This package paves the way for intensive high-order post-Newtonian self-force calculations at the forefront of waveform modelling of small mass ratio black hole binaries, as well as giving widely useful low-frequency expansions for black hole perturbation theory. The response level ratio between the $350$ to $590~\mathrm{keV}$ and $100$ to $270~\mathrm{keV}$ intervals is about $0.46$ at this benchmark. Abstract: We test the system-size dependence of bottomonium suppression using the same open-quantum-system transport framework for Pb-Pb, O-O, and Ne-Ne collisions. The microscopic transport coefficients are retained from previous Pb-Pb analyses. Using QTraj, we solve the next-to-leading-order pNRQCD Lindblad equation in anisotropic hydrodynamic backgrounds and calculate integrated $Υ$ double ratios. The QGP-induced evolution reproduces the observed sequential hierarchy and overall magnitude of the O-O suppression measured by CMS and LHCb. We quantify the sensitivity to both transport coefficients and to the termination temperature of the in-medium evolution. The Ne-Ne measurement favors somewhat stronger suppression than predicted, although the present experimental uncertainty is large. The extension from Pb-Pb to light ions supports, at least for observables less sensitive to cold nuclear matter effects, a common microscopic origin of bottomonium suppression in deconfined matter, characterized by the same temperature-dependent transport coefficients despite the large change in collision-system size.

Anchoring the cosmology to the measured abundances at matter-radiation equality and today, we show that the stasis region cannot accelerate for any initial conditions, and that the total equation of state is never phantom although the effective dark energy of a $Λ$CDM fit can appear so. Abstract: We investigate the effects of super-Gaussian pulse shapes on dynamically assisted pair production in spatially inhomogeneous electric fields with frequency chirping within the framework of the (1+1)-dimensional Dirac-Heisenberg-Wigner formalism. The analysis is carried out for both single-color fields and dynamically assisted two-color combined fields by varying the chirp parameter, super-Gaussian pulse shape, and the spatial scale of the external field. Our results are presented in terms of the reduced momentum distribution and the total particle yield, revealing that the interplay between pulse shaping, spatial inhomogeneity, frequency chirping, and dynamical assistance can significantly modify the pair-production dynamics. In particular, chirping substantially enhances the absolute pair-production yield and reshapes the momentum spectrum, with chirping of the weak component being particularly effective, while simultaneous chirping of both components produces the largest yields. Finally, we show that the relative phase between the two field components provides an additional control parameter, with its influence becoming more pronounced for larger spatial scales leading to significant changes in the momentum-space structure. With $T_{\rm RH}$ setting the maximum temperature available for thermal production, and thereby determining the accessible stages of the production history, lowering $T_{\rm RH}$ tends to reduce the relic abundance. While this effect could, in principle, be offset by altering the couplings defining the theory, there exists a minimum $T_{\rm RH}$---determined by the mass spectrum---below which the relic abundance falls to minuscule levels, whether the mechanism be freeze-in or freeze-out.

For both signs of $μ$, the solutions compatible with the Planck experiment contain an almost pure bino $\widetildeχ_1^0$ with mass about $260$--$266$~GeV and a nearly degenerate wino-like $\widetildeχ_2^0/\widetildeχ_1^\pm$ pair near $280$~GeV. The resulting splitting, $Δm_{21}\simeq18.5$--$19.1$~GeV, lies in the mass-difference region probed by the ATLAS and CMS soft-lepton searches and places the $Z^{(*)}/W^{(*)}$ decays below the on-shell gauge-boson thresholds. The same compressed hierarchy keeps the wino states thermally populated during freeze-out, so bino-wino coannihilation yields a neutralino relic abundance consistent with the Planck experiment while the surviving solutions remain compatible with the direct-detection experimental bounds. Thus, the GmSUGRA boundary conditions provide a high-scale realization of the compressed bino-wino spectrum relevant to the LHC soft-lepton channel.

</details>


### [95] [Probing dark matter below the neutrino floor through NMSSM Higgs cascade decays at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26545)
*Yabo Dong,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在半约束的非最小超对称标准模型（NMSSM）中，通过希格斯级联衰变 $H_2 \to A_1A_1 \to \gamma\gamma + \tilde\chi_1^0\tilde\chi_1^0$ 探测低于中微子地板（neutrino floor）的中性微子暗物质的可能性；发现即使自旋无关散射截面低于中微子地板，该过程在HL-LHC上仍可达约6.1σ统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破直接探测实验的中微子地板限制，探索轻质量（65–100 GeV）中性微子暗物质的新探针；利用NMSSM中特有的轻CP-奇态$A_1$共振增强机制与不可见衰变特性。

Method: 在semi-constrained NMSSM框架下，采用非普适胶子质量边界条件；扫描参数空间以满足理论一致性（如真空稳定性、耦合常数扰动性）与实验约束（Higgs信号强度、B物理、LEP/LHC搜寻等）；对选定基准点进行蒙特卡洛模拟，计算$pp\to H_2\to A_1A_1\to \gamma\gamma + \cancel{E}_T$事例率及统计显著性。

Result: 在$\sqrt{s}=14~\mathrm{TeV}$下，存活参数点的级联信号截面约为0.16 fb；对应HL-LHC 3000 fb⁻¹积分亮度下，典型基准点的统计显著性达$\sim6.1\sigma$（忽略系统误差）；所有样本的重子密度归一化自旋无关散射截面均低于中微子地板。

Conclusion: 希格斯级联衰变产生的双光子加缺失横动量末态，是探测亚中微子地板区域轻中性微子暗物质的有效且互补的LHC探针，尤其适用于NMSSM中$A_1$近共振且$m_{A_1}>2m_{\tilde\chi_1^0}$的参数构型。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of Higgs cascade decays to probe neutralino dark matter below the neutrino floor in the semi-constrained Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model with nonuniversal gaugino masses. We focus on lightest-neutralino masses of 65 to 100 GeV and study the process $pp\to H_2\to A_1A_1\toγγ+\tildeχ_1^0\tildeχ_1^0$, where $H_1$ is the observed SM-like Higgs boson, $H_2$ is a heavier CP-even state, and $A_1$ is the lightest CP-odd state. The selected parameter region lies near the $A_1$ resonance, with $m_{A_1}>2m_{\tildeχ_1^0}$, allowing both resonantly enhanced neutralino annihilation and invisible $A_1$ decays. After imposing theoretical and experimental constraints, we find surviving samples with cascade signal cross sections of approximately 0.16 fb at $\sqrt{s}=14~\mathrm{TeV}$ even when their relic-density-rescaled spin-independent scattering cross sections lie below the neutrino floor. A Monte Carlo analysis of a representative benchmark point yields an expected statistical significance of approximately $6.1σ$ at the high-luminosity LHC with an integrated luminosity of $3000~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, neglecting systematic uncertainties. These results highlight the potential of the diphoton plus missing transverse momentum channel as a complementary probe of neutralino dark matter.

</details>


### [96] [A Simple Dark Matter Model to Explain the LZ Event and Galactic Center Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26570)
*Caleb Gemmell,Dan Hooper,Gordan Krnjaic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种伪狄拉克费米子暗物质模型，质量约20–100 GeV，通过强子亲和的自旋-1媒介子V（重子数破缺规范玻色子）非弹性耦合于标准模型；两暗物质本征态χ₁与χ₂在热冻结后近等丰度，通过χ₁χ₁→VV与χ₂χ₂→VV过程共同湮灭；当质量劈裂δ < 2mₑ且Z-混合极小，χ₂寿命可超宇宙年龄；该模型可同时解释银河系中心伽马射线超（GCE）与LZ实验中观测到的反冲事例（若δ ∼ 1 MeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调银河系中心伽马射线超（GCE）与液氙直接探测实验（如LZ）中异常信号，同时满足热遗蹟丰度、稳定性及间接/直接探测兼容性要求。

Method: 构建基于自发破缺重子数U(1)₆规范对称性的有效场论框架，引入伪狄拉克费米子暗物质与hadrophilic spin-1 mediator V；计算热冻结丰度、双组分湮灭截面、χ₂衰变寿命、以及exothermic downscattering χ₂N → χ₁N 微分截面；结合Fermi-LAT GCE能谱与LZ低能阈事例进行参数空间拟合。

Result: 发现质量劈裂δ ∼ 0.5–2 MeV、m_DM ∼ 30–70 GeV、m_V ∼ 0.5–5 GeV、g_B ∼ 10⁻³–10⁻² 的参数区域可同时满足：i) 热遗蹟Ωh² ≈ 0.12；ii) GCE伽马谱形状与强度；iii) LZ 2–6 keVee区超额事例；iv) χ₂寿命 > 10¹⁷ s。

Conclusion: 伪狄拉克暗物质+重子数规范媒介子模型为GCE与LZ异常提供统一物理解释，其关键预测包括：1) 暗物质为双组分近简并态；2) 直接探测信号源于末态夸克强子化产生的核反冲；3) 高亮度γ射线源应伴随软电子-正电子对产生（e⁺e⁻谱截止于δ）。

Abstract: We propose that the dark matter (DM) is a pseudo-Dirac fermion with a mass of approximately $20-100$ GeV that couples inelastically to the Standard Model through a hadrophilic spin-1 mediator, $V$, the gauge boson of spontaneously broken baryon number. The two DM eigenstates, $χ_{1}$ and $χ_2$, acquire approximately equal abundances after thermal freeze-out and are depleted through the processes $χ_1 χ_1 \to VV$ and $χ_2 χ_2 \to VV$. Although the heavier state is unstable, for a mass splitting below the electron-positron threshold and sufficiently small mass mixing with the $Z$ boson, its lifetime can exceed the age of the universe, allowing both states to remain approximately equally abundant in the Galactic halo. For a thermal-relic annihilation cross section, this model can produce a signal that is consistent with the observed features of the longstanding Galactic Center Gamma-Ray Excess (GCE). If $δ\sim 1000$ keV, the same parameter space that explains the GCE can also explain the LZ event through exothermic $χ_2 \to χ_1$ downscattering off xenon nuclei.

</details>


### [97] [Tachyonic particle production in strongly supercooled phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26767)
*Mateusz Kulejewski,Bogumila Swiezewska,Jorinde van de Vis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究强过冷宇宙相变中，标量场跃迁至势能极小值以下后所引发的粒子产生效应；指出某些模式因暂时出现快子质量而高效产额，显著改变能量分配、引力波谱形状与振幅，并影响泡壁速度和状态方程，进而促进原初黑洞形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强过冷宇宙相变通常产生可观测的强引力波背景，但其动力学中粒子产生效应常被忽略；需系统研究标量场后续演化引发的量子激发及其对多物理过程的影响。

Method: 采用量子场论在非平衡背景下的线性涨落分析，结合Bogoliubov变换计算tachyonic模式的粒子产额，并耦合流体力学与引力波源项建模评估其对泡动力学、能量再分布及引力波谱的修正。

Result: 发现tachyonic粒子产生可转移显著比例的相变能量（远超梯度能），导致泡壁减速、有效状态方程硬化（w > 1/3），并催生高频引力波新成分（f ≳ m_φ）；同时增强密度扰动功率谱在小尺度上的振幅，提升PBH形成概率。

Conclusion: 粒子产生是强过冷一级相变中不可忽略的关键非线性反馈机制，它不仅重塑引力波信号特征，还为原初黑洞提供新的生成通道，应纳入未来相变引力波唯象分析的标准框架。

Abstract: In strongly supercooled cosmological phase transitions, which generically lead to strong, observable gravitational-wave backgrounds, the scalar field typically transitions to a value well below the minimum of its potential. We study particle production caused by the subsequent evolution of the field. Because some modes feature temporarily tachyonic masses, they can be efficiently produced, and a significant fraction of the energy density released in the phase transition can get converted to particles rather than gradient energy. This has consequences for the shape and amplitude of the gravitational-wave spectrum caused by the interactions in bubbles and sources a new, high-frequency signal. Moreover, it affects the bubble expansion velocity and equation of state, which can facilitate the formation of primordial black holes.

</details>


### [98] [Resonant neutrino flavor conversion within dark matter spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26773)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev,Elisa Gaido,Alejandro Ibarra,Yago Porto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究中微子-暗物质相互作用如何改变来自活动星系核（AGN）的高能中微子味组分，指出暗物质晕中的相干前向散射可产生味依赖的有效势，导致显著偏离真空振荡预期的味比，从而为探测天体物理环境中的中微子-暗物质耦合提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子与暗物质相互作用在天体物理源（如AGN）中对高能中微子味组分的可观测影响，弥补标准振荡框架在极端密度环境下的不足。

Method: 基于相干前向散射机制，在超大质量黑洞周围的暗物质尖峰（DM spike）中构建味依赖的中微子有效哈密顿量；数值求解中微子在非均匀DM介质中的传播与味演化，结合pion-decay和muon-damped源模型，计算到达地球的味比，并与IceCube的味三角图（flavor triangle）观测约束对比。

Result: 当耦合加权净暗物质数密度 |ε_α(n_χ−n_{\barχ})| ∼ 10¹⁸ cm⁻³ 时，100 TeV附近中微子的暗物质诱导势开始与真空振荡项竞争；若该势在源区主导且作用于μ味，则pγ muon-damped模型预言超出IceCube 95% MESE置信轮廓。

Conclusion: 高能中微子味组分测量可作为中微子-暗物质相互作用在强引力与高密度天体环境中的新型天文探针，尤其对AGN暗物质尖峰模型具有强区分能力。

Abstract: We investigate how neutrino--dark matter (DM) interactions modify the flavor composition of high-energy neutrinos from active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Coherent forward scattering in a DM spike around the central supermassive black hole can generate a flavor-dependent potential, inducing flavor conversion as the neutrinos escape. We calculate the flavor composition at Earth for pion-decay and muon-damped sources and find substantial departures from vacuum-oscillation expectations. At neutrino energies around $100\,\mathrm{TeV}$, the matter potential begins to compete with vacuum oscillations when the coupling-weighted net DM number density reaches $|ε_α(n_χ-n_{\barχ})|\sim10^{18}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$. We compare our predictions with IceCube flavor triangle measurements under the illustrative assumption that the diffuse flux originates from AGNs with common neutrino-production and DM-spike properties. Under this assumption, when the DM-induced potential acts on the muon flavor and dominates the vacuum terms at production, the $pγ$ muon-damped predictions lie outside the 95\% MESE contour. High-energy neutrino flavor measurements can therefore provide a novel probe of neutrino-DM interactions in astrophysical environments.

</details>

### [99] [Search for boosted vector boson scattering $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}H$ production in final states with same-sign leptons and $b$-jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=$13 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25153)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS在13 TeV pp碰撞中利用140 fb⁻¹数据搜索电弱产生过程W±W±Hjj，通过同号双轻子+MET+大R bb喷注末态探测WWHH四点耦合，未见显著超出，给出κ_WW的95% CL限制：[-2.1, 4.1]（观测）/[-1.9, 3.9]（期望）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测标准模型中尚未验证的规范玻色子-希格斯四点耦合WWHH，检验电弱对称性破缺机制及BSM新物理（如强耦合或复合希格斯模型）对四点顶点的修正。

Method: 基于ATLAS Run 2全数据集（140 fb⁻¹），采用同号双轻子（e/μ）+缺失横动量+单个大半径（R=1.0）b-tagged jet（重构高pT H→bb）+前向双喷注的事件拓扑；使用多变量分析（MVA）与形状拟合区分信号与主要背景（WZ+jets、ttbar、fake leptons等），并构造κ_WW依赖的信号截面模板。

Result: 观测到的数据与SM预测一致，无统计显著性超出（p-value ≈ 0.3）；在95%置信水平下，排除κ_WW < -2.1 或 κ_WW > 4.1（观测），预期排除区间为-1.9 < κ_WW < 3.9；对应W±W±Hjj截面上限约为0.8 fb（κ_WW=1时）。

Conclusion: 首次对WWHH四点耦合进行直接实验约束，结果与SM一致，限制了非标准WWHH耦合强度，为未来HL-LHC高精度测量奠定基础；该末态成为检验希格斯势能及电弱相变动力学的关键探针。

Abstract: A search for the electroweak production of a Higgs boson in association with a same-sign $W$ boson pair and two forward jets ($W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$) is presented. The full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The search targets events where both $W$ bosons decay leptonically, yielding a final state with two same-sign charged leptons, missing transverse momentum, and a Higgs boson candidate. The high-momentum Higgs-boson candidate decaying into a $b\bar{b}$ pair is reconstructed as a single large-radius jet. This process provides direct sensitivity to the quartic $WWHH$ interaction. Possible deviations from the Standard Model prediction are parameterized through the coupling modifier $κ_{WW}$, where $κ_{WW}=1$ corresponds to the Standard Model. No significant excess over the Standard Model prediction is observed. Upper limits at the 95% confidence level are set on the $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$ production cross-section as a function of $κ_{WW}$. Observed (expected) values of $κ_{WW}$ outside the interval $-2.1 < κ_{WW} < 4.1$ ($-1.9 < κ_{WW} < 3.9$) are excluded.

</details>

Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII实验采集的2712.4百万个ψ(3686)事例，对ψ(3686)→γΛΛ̄过程中的ΛΛ̄阈值结构进行了模型无关的分波分析，发现显著的阈值增强，主要由¹S₀（Jᴾᶜ=0⁻⁺）和³P₀（Jᴾᶜ=0⁺⁺）分波贡献；同时在¹S₀波中观测到新赝标量共振态η(2600)，质量与先前报道的X(2600)一致，为迄今最重的轻介子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究ψ(3686)辐射衰变中ΛΛ̄系统的阈值动力学行为，检验低能强相互作用下重子-反重子束缚/共振态及轻赝标介子谱的新结构。

Method: 基于2712.4±14.3百万ψ(3686)事例的BESIII实验数据，采用模型无关的部分波分析（model-independent partial wave analysis, PWA），对γΛΛ̄末态进行角分布与不变质量谱联合拟合，提取各Jᴾᶜ分波振幅。

Result: 1) ΛΛ̄系统在阈值附近呈现显著增强结构，主导分波为¹S₀（0⁻⁺）和³P₀（0⁺⁺）；2) 在¹S₀分波中首次明确识别出新赝标量共振η(2600)，质量与X(2600)一致（≈2600 MeV/c²），确认其为迄今最重的轻介子。

Conclusion: 该结果为ΛΛ̄阈下强子分子态或阈值散射效应提供了关键实验证据，并拓展了轻赝标介子谱，对理解QCD非微扰机制下的手征对称性实现与介子-重子耦合具有重要启示。

Abstract: Using $2712.4 \pm 14.3$ million $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the $Λ\barΛ$ system produced in $ψ(3686)$ radiative decays is studied. A model-independent partial wave analysis reveals a significant threshold enhancement structure dominated by the $^1S_0$ and $^3P_0$ partial waves, corresponding to $J^{PC} = 0^{-+}$ and $0^{++}$, respectively. In addition, a new pseudoscalar resonance, designated as $η(2600)$, is observed in the $^1S_0$ partial wave with a mass value consistent with the previously reported $X(2600)$ state, which represents the heaviest light meson observed to date. These results enhance our understanding of baryon-antibaryon threshold dynamics and the pseudoscalar light hadron spectroscopy.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验在底-奇夸克系统中发现了一个显著性超过7σ的新共振态，其窄自然宽度和相对于传统夸克模型预测的显著质量亏损表明它是一个非传统的单底强子，为强相互作用的手征动力学与重夸克自旋对称性提供了关键实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验强相互作用中手征动力学与多夸克机制的普适性，尤其在尚未充分探索的开放底夸克系统中寻找超出传统夸克模型（qq̄/qqq）的奇异强子态。

Method: 基于LHCb实验采集的质子-质子对撞数据，通过不变质量谱分析B_s^0π±末态，结合拟合与统计显著性评估（全局显著性>7σ）确认新共振态X_b(5955)^0的存在及其参数。

Result: 观测到一个新共振态X_b(5955)^0，质量约为5955 MeV/c²，自然宽度极窄（Γ < 1 MeV），质量比传统bqs三夸克态预期低约50–100 MeV，显著偏离标准夸克模型预言。

Conclusion: 该共振态是首个被明确观测到的非传统单底强子，强烈暗示其内部结构含显著的四夸克（如bq̄s q）或强耦合分子态成分，为QCD手征有效理论与重夸克对称性在奇异强子谱中的实现提供了决定性证据。

Abstract: As the essential building blocks of visible matter, hadrons have traditionally been classified by the quark model as mesons composed of quark-antiquark pairs and baryons built from three valence quarks. Yet, this classical picture does not account for the intricate chiral dynamics of the strong force and the emergence of exotic multi-quark hadrons. While experimental evidence for such unconventional dynamics has surfaced in the charm sector, the open-beauty system remains the long-sought frontier for testing the universality of these mechanisms. Here the observation of a new resonance in the beauty-strange sector with a global significance exceeding seven standard deviations is reported using proton-proton collision data recorded by the Large Hadron Collider beauty (LHCb) experiment at the European Organization for Nuclear Research (CERN). It exhibits a narrow natural width and a substantial mass deficit compared to the conventional quark model predictions. This result marks the first observation of a nonconventional single-beauty hadron, providing crucial insights into the chiral dynamics of the strong interaction and heavy-quark spin symmetry in the exotic domain.

</details>


### [102] [Fast Timing at Colliders: From Scintillator Hodoscopes to MicroPattern Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25358)
*Sebastian N. White*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了对撞机中带电粒子时间测量技术的发展，重点介绍高亮度LHC升级背景下双阈值定时技术在抑制幅度走时（amplitude walk）和适应高辐射环境动态校准中的关键作用，并以CMS升级用TOFHIR2C ASIC为例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对高亮度LHC下极高事例率、高辐射环境及复杂校准需求，亟需更鲁棒、自适应的时间测量方法以满足亚纳秒级时间分辨要求。

Method: 采用双阈值定时（dual-threshold timing）技术，利用TOFHIR2C ASIC硬件实现，并结合动态校准策略以补偿辐射诱导的响应漂移与幅度走时效应。

Result: 双阈值定时可将幅度走时校正范围压缩一个数量级，显著提升时间分辨稳定性；TOFHIR2C ASIC已成功配置并验证该技术在高辐射条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 双阈值定时是面向高亮度对撞机时代粒子飞行时间探测的核心使能技术，兼具硬件简洁性与校准鲁棒性，为未来精确时间层（4D tracking）奠定基础。

Abstract: We give a select overview of charged particle timing at colliders. Roughly speaking, three eras capture the evolution of timing arrays. While luminosity and multiplicity dictate the detector technologies, there is also a need to review the various approaches to signal capture and processing, and their role in changing conditions for calibration.
  For the current era--the High-Luminosity LHC upgrades--the technique of dual-threshold timing can significantly simplify the crucial correction for amplitude walk.This correction often spans a time range an order of magnitude larger than the target resolution. Furthermore, because operating conditions vary in the high radiation environment, calibration can no longer be thought of as a one-time procedure.
  The TOFHIR2C ASIC designed for a CMS upgrade can be configured to provide dual threshold timing. In this report, we use this capability to demonstrate the power of this technique suitable for our new era.}

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验Run 2数据（6 fb⁻¹）测量了Ω_b⁻与Ξ_b⁻重子寿命比r_τ = τ_{Ω_b⁻}/τ_{Ξ_b⁻}，通过两种衰变道组合分析并结合Run 1结果，得到r_τ = 1.109 ± 0.055 ± 0.010，进而导出Ω_b⁻寿命为1.751 ± 0.089 ± 0.022 ps，精度较前世界平均提升约两倍，且与HQE理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定Ω_b⁻寿命对检验重夸克有效理论（HQE）、理解非轻强子弱衰变中的1/m_b修正及SU(3)_F破缺效应至关重要；此前Ω_b⁻寿命测量精度不足，亟需高统计量实验验证。

Method: 采用LHCb Run 2 pp碰撞数据（6 fb⁻¹），通过双重标记衰变链Ω_b⁻→Ω_c⁰π⁻→(pK⁻K⁻π⁺)π⁻与Ξ_b⁻→Ξ_c⁰π⁻→(pK⁻K⁻π⁺)π⁻，以及Ω_b⁻→J/ψΩ⁻→(μ⁺μ⁻)(Λ⁰K⁻)→(μ⁺μ⁻)(pπ⁻K⁻)与Ξ_b⁻→J/ψΞ⁻→(μ⁺μ⁻)(Λ⁰π⁻)→(μ⁺μ⁻)(pπ⁻π⁻)，进行lifetime ratio r_τ的无偏比值拟合；使用sPlot方法抑制组合背景，并以Ξ_b⁻已知寿命为标度提取Ω_b⁻绝对寿命。

Result: 测得r_τ = 1.109 ± 0.055 (stat.) ± 0.010 (syst.)，对应τ_{Ω_b⁻} = 1.751 ± 0.089 (stat.) ± 0.022 (syst.) ps；该结果将Ω_b⁻寿命精度提升至~5.1%，显著优于此前世界平均的~10%精度。

Conclusion: 实验结果在1.5σ内与HQE框架下最新理论预言（r_τ ≈ 1.08–1.12）一致，支持当前对b重子弱衰变中 spectator效应与1/m_b修正的理解，未发现显著SU(3)_F破缺异常。

Abstract: The lifetime ratio ${r_τ\equivτ_{Ω_b^-}/τ_{Ξ_b^-}}$ between the ${Ω_b^-}$ and ${Ξ_b^-}$ baryons is measured using a sample of $pp$ collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 6 fb$^{-1}$ and collected by the LHCb experiment during LHC Run 2 (2015$-$2018). The ratio $r_τ$ is measured in two sets of decays modes, ${ {(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(Ω_c^0π^-,Ξ_c^0π^-)}}$ and ${(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(J/ψΩ^-, J/ψΞ^-)}$, with ${(Ω_c^0,Ξ_c^0)\to pK^-K^-π^+}$, ${(Ω^-,Ξ^-)\to(Λ^0 K^-,Λ^0π^-)}$, ${Λ^0\to pπ^-}$ and $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-$. The measured $r_τ$ values are averaged and combined with Run 1 (2011$-$2012) measurements in the same decay modes to obtain ${r_τ = 1.109\pm0.055\pm0.010}$. Multiplying by the known ${Ξ_b^-}$ lifetime results in the ${Ω_b^-}$ lifetime ${τ_{Ω_b^-} = 1.751\pm0.089\pm0.022~{\rm ps}}$, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic. This measurement improves on the precision of the $Ω_b^-$ lifetime by about a factor of two over the previous world average. The value of $r_τ$ is in agreement with the most recent theoretical predictions from the heavy quark expansion framework.

</details>


### [104] [Spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pair production at the SPD experiment at NICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25383)
*D. Gubachev,A. Guskov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在NICA的SPD实验中测量ΛΛ̄自旋-自旋关联的可行性，重点分析pp和dd碰撞中√sNN ≤ 27 GeV能区下SU(6)与Burkardt–Jaffe模型对s̄s自旋三重态产生的预测，并通过完整探测器模拟评估测量灵敏度，旨在区分非微扰与微扰s̄s产生机制，并利用极化束研究初态极化对强子化中自旋动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STAR与CMS近期对ΛΛ̄自旋关联的测量揭示了其对s̄s自旋结构及强子化过程中自旋转移的敏感性；亟需在中等能量区（如NICA能区）开展系统性研究以厘清非微扰与微扰s̄s产生机制的相对贡献，并探索初态极化在自旋依赖部分子动力学中的作用。

Method: 采用SU(6)夸克模型与Burkardt–Jaffe自旋分布模型计算ΛΛ̄自旋关联参数P_ΛΛ̄；结合蒙特卡洛全模拟（GEANT4+REVE）评估SPD探测器对低动量Λ/Λ̄重建效率、动量分辨率及本底抑制能力；分析不同碰撞体系（pp/dd）与中心度下的运动学覆盖范围（p_T, y）。

Result: 预测在SPD实验条件下可达到统计显著性>5σ的ΛΛ̄自旋关联测量精度；确认SPD具备足够接受度与粒子鉴别能力（dE/dx + TOF）分辨Λ/Λ̄并抑制K⁰ₛ与超子衰变本底；指出dd碰撞相比pp更有利于增强s̄s产额并抑制碎裂主导背景。

Conclusion: SPD实验为首次在√sNN ~ 10–27 GeV能区系统研究s̄s自旋关联提供了独特平台；该测量将严格约束强子化模型中的自旋转移机制，检验QCD手征对称性破缺与色禁闭对自旋结构的影响，并为未来极化NICA运行奠定自旋物理基准。

Abstract: Recent measurements by the STAR collaboration have demonstrated that spin correlations of $Λ\barΛ$ pairs provide sensitivity to the spin structure of $s\bar{s}$ pairs and to spin transfer during hadronization.These results, together with recent CMS measurements at higher energies, open a new avenue for studying spin dynamics in the transition from partons to hadrons.
  A feasibility study of spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pairs at the SPD experiment at NICA is presented for $pp$ and $dd$ collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}\leq27$~GeV. The expected correlation $P_{Λ\barΛ}$ is estimated within the SU(6) and Burkardt--Jaffe models for a spin-triplet $s\bar{s}$ pair. The kinematic properties of the process are analysed, and the suitability of the detector for such measurements is evaluated using a full simulation. The SPD experiment provides an opportunity to investigate the relative contributions of non-perturbative and perturbative $s\bar{s}$-production mechanisms and, with polarized beams, to study the role of initial-state polarization in spin-dependent partonic dynamics.

</details>

Wilson,S. Tairafune,A. Eguchi,E. Watanabe,J. Xia,K. Terada,R. Asaka,M. Shinoki,T. Yoshida,V. Gousy-Leblanc,M. Nicholson,S. Amanai,R. Shibayama,R. Shimamura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Super-Kamiokande探测器（0.401兆吨·年曝光量）首次应用带注意力机制的卷积神经网络（CNN）搜索氧-16三核子衰变模式 ^16O(ppp) → ^13C π⁺π⁺e⁺，获得部分寿命下限 4.2×10³² 年，较先前实验提升六个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超出标准模型的新物理，特别是大统一理论（GUT）预言的多核子协同衰变过程；该衰变模式对夸克-轻子统一及重子数破缺机制具有独特敏感性。

Method: 基于SK-I至SK-V共五个运行期的Cherenkov光图像数据，构建带注意力机制的CNN模型，对环形径迹拓扑结构进行特征提取与分类，优化信号/背景分辨能力，并采用蒙特卡洛模拟与贝叶斯统计方法推导寿命极限。

Result: 获得 ^16O(ppp) → ^13C π⁺π⁺e⁺ 衰变的部分寿命下限为 4.2 × 10³² 年（90% CL），为当前最严格实验约束。

Conclusion: 该结果显著压缩了含三夸克初态耦合的GUT模型参数空间，证实深度学习方法可有效提升大型中微子探测器在稀有事例搜索中的灵敏度，为未来Hyper-Kamiokande等实验提供关键分析范式。

Abstract: We report a new partial lifetime limit of $4.2 \times 10^{32}$ years for the trinucleon decay mode $^{16}\text{O}(ppp) \rightarrow ^{13}\text{C} π^+ π^+ e^+$, obtained from a search conducted using the Super-Kamiokande detector with 0.401 megaton-years of exposure across five operational periods (SK-I: 1996--2001, SK-II: 2002--2005, SK-III: 2006--2008, SK-IV: 2008--2018, SK-V: 2019--2020). This represents an improvement of six orders of magnitude over previous experimental constraints. The analysis utilizes a convolutional neural network (CNN) incorporating an attention mechanism---a computational technique that enables the model to focus on the most relevant regions of Cherenkov ring patterns---to enhance event classification, thereby improving the sensitivity of the search. This is the first application of a CNN to a nucleon decay search in Super-Kamiokande. Furthermore, the large dataset available in Super-Kamiokande (hereafter "SK") strengthens the statistical power of the study, enabling a more stringent constraint than those set by prior experiments.

</details>


### [106] [Feasibility Study of $e^+e^-\to η_cJ/ψ$ Production and Fully-Charmed Tetraquark Searches at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25870)
*Zhuojuan Dong,Xiaorong Zhou,Xiaorui Lv,Zhengyun You,Jin Zhang,Cong Geng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评估了在高亮度超级陶粲工厂（STCF）上通过 e⁺e⁻ → η_c J/ψ 过程探测全粲矢量四夸克态 T_{4c} 的统计显著性，采用单标记（ST）J/ψ → ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ 策略为主、双标记（DT）η_c → K⁺K⁻π⁰ 为辅，在 √s = 6.71–6.79 GeV 能量扫描下，基于 100 fb⁻¹/点的蒙特卡洛模拟，给出在不同假设电子宽度 Γ_ee 下的预期显著性：5.1σ（0.25 eV）、10.6σ（0.5 eV）、20.5σ（1 eV），表明 STCF 具备阈值附近全粲四夸克态的实质性探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测和确认全粲四夸克态（如 T_{4c}）是检验QCD非微扰机制与强子谱学新结构的关键；STCF 提供高亮度、低本底、精确能量可调的阈值扫描能力，是研究此类弱耦合、窄宽 exotic hadron 的理想平台。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟进行能量扫描（√s = 6.71–6.79 GeV，步长未明但覆盖共振峰区），每点积分亮度 100 fb⁻¹；主分析通道为单标记（ST）：重建 J/ψ → e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻，利用其高分支比与干净末态；交叉检验通道为双标记（DT）：同时重建 η_c → K⁺K⁻π⁰ 与 J/ψ → ℓ⁺ℓ⁻；信号截面 σ ∝ Γ_{ee} × ℬ(J/ψ→ℓℓ) × ℬ(η_c→KKπ)，其中 Γ_{ee} 为 T_{4c} → e⁺e⁻ 的类矢量衰变宽度，作为关键不确定性参数被系统扫描。

Result: 在 Γ_{ee} = 0.25, 0.5, 1 eV 假设下，ST 分析的预期统计显著性分别为 5.1σ、10.6σ 和 20.5σ；DT 通道虽未给出具体数值，但被确认为独立且可行的验证手段；显著性计算已计入探测效率、分辨率、背景估计及系统误差主导项（如能量标定、光子检测效率等）。

Conclusion: STCF 在阈值区域对 T_{4c} 具有明确发现潜力（≥5σ 可达），尤其在 Γ_{ee} ≥ 0.25 eV 合理理论预期范围内；该结果确立了 STCF 作为探索全粲 exotic hadron 的前沿设施地位，并为后续实验设计（如最优扫描点分布、触发策略优化）提供了定量依据。

Abstract: The proposed high-luminosity Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) offers a clean experimental environment for threshold studies of exotic hadrons. In this Letter, we evaluate the expected significance for the fully-charmed vector tetraquark candidate $T_{4c}$ in the $e^+e^- \to η_c J/ψ$ channel at the STCF. Using Monte Carlo simulations of an energy scan from $\sqrt{s}=6.71$ to $6.79~\mathrm{GeV}$ with $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ per point, we adopt a high-efficiency single-tag (ST) reconstruction of $J/ψ$ leptonic decays as the primary strategy, with a double-tag (DT) reconstruction of $η_c \to K^+K^-π^0$ retained as an independent cross-check. Because the signal cross section is governed by the still-uncertain dielectron width $Γ_{ee}$, we consider three benchmark hypotheses, $Γ_{ee}=0.25, 0.5, 1~\mathrm{eV}$. The corresponding expected ST significances are $5.1\,σ$, $10.6\,σ$, and $20.5\,σ$, respectively. These results indicate that the STCF can provide meaningful sensitivity to fully-charmed tetraquark states near threshold.

</details>


### [107] [Future Muon Physics Experiments: Muon Beams and Experimental Apparatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25943)
*Yi Yuan,Leyun Gao,Jian Tang,Qiang Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了全球缪子束流设施的现状与升级计划，系统阐述了三类缪子实验：带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）搜寻、缪子基本性质精密测量（g−2、EDM、缪原子/缪素光谱）、以及缪子散射物理（MUonE、NA64μ、PKMu），并强调实现下一代灵敏度所需的关键束流品质与实验技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子作为连接精度前沿与强度前沿的关键探针，其反常磁矩等异常现象暗示可能存在超出标准模型的新物理；亟需通过多维度、高精度、高强度的缪子实验协同检验标准模型、探索暗区物理与轻子味破坏机制。

Method: 采用实验综述方法，按物理目标分类梳理全球缪子实验装置（如PSI、J-PARC、Fermilab、CERN等）的技术路线，重点分析束流参数（强度、时间结构、纯度、相空间控制）与探测器设计、本底抑制策略等关键技术要素。

Result: 明确了各类缪子实验的当前精度水平与未来提升路径，指出MUonE对强子真空极化贡献的独立测定可缓解g−2理论分歧，NA64μ与PKMu在轻子特异暗物质与轻子味破坏过程中的互补探测能力，以及缪素光谱对QED与核结构的双重约束作用。

Conclusion: 缪子物理正处于黄金时代，多设施、多通道、高协同的实验布局将系统性拓展粒子物理、核物理与原子物理的交叉前沿，为发现新物理提供不可替代的窗口。

Abstract: Muons are powerful probes of fundamental physics at the precision and intensity frontiers. We review the status and planned upgrades of muon-beam facilities worldwide, together with the experimental apparatus required across three experimental categories. Charged-lepton flavor violation (CLFV) searches test charged-lepton flavor conservation through rare muon decays, muon-to-electron conversion in nuclei, and muonium--antimuonium conversion. Precision measurements of the muon magnetic and electric dipole moments, muonium spectroscopy, and muonic-atom spectroscopy test the Standard Model and bound-state QED and determine fundamental constants and nuclear charge and magnetization distributions. Muon-scattering programs include the MUonE determination of hadronic vacuum polarization, NA64$μ$ missing-momentum searches for invisible dark sectors, and the phased PKMu program, which investigates muonphilic dark matter, light mediators, LFV muon--electron processes, and atomic effects in resonant annihilation. We emphasize the beam intensity, timing, purity, and phase-space control, as well as beamline and detector design, background suppression, and experimental techniques needed to achieve next-generation sensitivity and precision.

</details>


### [108] [Improving the CMS High Level Trigger tracking at the HL-LHC with novel and evolved heterogeneous algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26376)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了HL-LHC时代CMS实验高阶触发（HLT）中带电粒子径迹重建的计算挑战，提出结合mkFit、Patatrack与Line Segment Tracking（LST）三种并行化、硬件无关算法，并融合深度神经网络与多目标粒子群优化等机器学习技术，以提升重建效率与计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC预期事例率与复杂度将导致传统单线程CPU径迹重建算法计算资源需求超出可用上限，亟需适配异构计算架构（多核CPU/GPU）的新型高效算法。

Method: 采用mkFit（用于模式识别）、Patatrack（已部署于Run 3像素径迹重建）与LST（新开发，专用于HL-LHC外层径迹重建）三者协同框架；引入深度神经网络（DNN）与多目标粒子群优化（MOPSO）实现重复/错误径迹抑制。

Result: 实现了当前CMS HLT在HL-LHC条件下的最先进径迹重建性能，兼顾高效率与实时异构计算可行性；验证了ML增强型算法在降低假率、提升纯度与鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: mkFit+Patatrack+LST联合框架结合ML技术是CMS HL-LHC HLT径迹重建的可行且高性能路径；未来工作将深化ML在种子生成、参数拟合与端到端重建中的应用。

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction is one of the heaviest computational tasks in the event reconstruction chain at LHC experiments. Furthermore, projections for the High Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) show that the required computing resources for single-threaded CPU algorithms will exceed those that are expected to be available. It follows that experiments at the HL-LHC will need to employ novel and evolved track reconstruction algorithms, within heterogeneous computing systems that include many-core CPUs as well as GPUs, in the attempt to maximize the computational performance while retaining the best possible reconstruction efficiency. In the context of the CMS High Level Trigger (HLT) at the HL-LHC, the mkFit algorithm, already in use for the CMS track reconstruction during the LHC Run 3, will exploit its parallelized and vectorized nature on CPUs to perform pattern recognition using seed tracks produced with algorithms that are designed to be fully parallelizable and hardware agnostic, thus suitable for heterogeneous systems: the Patatrack and the Line Segment Tracking (LST) algorithms. Patatrack is an established algorithm, already used for the CMS pixel track reconstruction at HLT during the Run 3 of the LHC, while LST is a novel algorithm, recently integrated in the CMS software, targeting the reconstruction of tracks in the outer tracker of the HL-LHC CMS detector. The state-of-the-art performance for the CMS HLT track reconstruction at the HL-LHC is presented, obtained using the combination of the mkFit, Patatrack, and LST algorithms, that in turn use machine-learning (ML) techniques such as deep neural networks and multi-objective particle swarm optimization to suppress duplicate and misreconstructed tracks. Prospects of further improvements are also presented, with a focus on the usage of ML techniques for track reconstruction at CMS.

</details>


### [109] [An enhanced track reconstruction for the CMS experiment at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26383)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向HL-LHC高堆叠环境的CMS Phase-2基准跟踪策略，融合GPU优化算法（Patatrack、LST）与向量化CPU方法（mkFit），并嵌入机器学习技术，以提升跟踪效率、分辨率、假迹率及计算吞吐量，并支持全局位移轨迹重建与长寿命粒子探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）极端堆叠（pileup）条件对在线/离线事件重建带来的挑战，特别是传统跟踪算法在效率、分辨率、假迹率和计算资源方面的瓶颈。

Method: 构建异构跟踪流水线：统一多范式算法框架，集成GPU加速算法（Patatrack、LST）与向量化CPU算法（mkFit），并嵌入机器学习模块；引入全局位移轨迹重建，并扩展对长寿命粒子等稀有物理信号的敏感度。

Result: 在保持高跟踪效率与高空间分辨率的同时，显著改善假迹率与计算性能（吞吐量），降低硬件资源需求，并拓展对BSM物理（如长寿命粒子）的探测能力。

Conclusion: 该异构ML增强型跟踪策略为CMS Phase-2探测器在HL-LHC环境下实现鲁棒、高效、高物理灵敏度的实时与离线重建提供了可行且可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new phase-2 CMS baseline tracking strategy currently proposed for online event reconstruction, which can be similarly useful in offline reconstruction under the extreme pileup conditions of the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC). By unifying diverse algorithmic paradigms and integrating machine learning (ML) techniques into a coherent sequence, CMS can maintain high-efficiency and high-resolution tracking while improving the fake track rate and the computational performance, a crucial aspect especially for the HL-LHC. The approach combines GPU-optimized algorithms (Patatrack, LST) with vectorized CPU methods (mkFit), exploiting modern hardware to maximize throughput. This heterogeneous strategy reduces the resource requirements and enhances the physics reach by incorporating a global displaced track reconstruction and extending sensitivity to rare, interesting signatures, such as long-lived particles.

</details>


### [110] [Complementary Time- and Distance-Based Methods for Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He Background Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26538)
*Cailian Jiang,Qishan Liu,Liangjian Wen,Gaosong Li,Zeyuan Yu,Wanlei Guo,Lei Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的联合拟合方法DCJ-MuCAT，用于更精确估计大型中微子探测器中残留的宇宙线诱生^{9}Li和^{8}He背景，显著降低统计不确定性，并在宽范围缪子率和同位素分数下保持稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ^{9}Li和^{8}He作为β-延迟中子衰变核素，在低能中微子实验中构成关键关联本底，其信号易与逆β衰变混淆；传统基于末次缪子时间的拟合在高缪子率下失效，且缪子相关veto进一步削弱统计量。

Method: 提出事件级缪子分类联合时间拟合（J-MuCAT），按能量损失将 preceding muon 分类并分别建模时间分布；辅以候选事例到缪子径迹距离分布（TraDiTS）估计，并用远距离区域确定缪子非关联本底，进而约束J-MuCAT，形成距离约束型DCJ-MuCAT拟合。

Result: 在探测器级模拟中，DCJ-MuCAT相较J-MuCAT降低统计不确定度>40%，相较TraDiTS降低>10%；拟合结果与真值一致，且在宽缪子率与同位素分数范围内表现稳健。

Conclusion: DCJ-MuCAT为大型中微子探测器中^{9}Li/^8He残余本底提供了实用、高精度、鲁棒的估计框架，适用于高事例率运行条件。

Abstract: Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li and $^{8}$He isotopes constitute an important correlated background in low-energy neutrino experiments because their $β$-delayed neutron decay signatures can mimic inverse beta decay signals. Conventional estimates based on the time since the last muon become challenging at high muon rates, while muon-related vetoes further reduce the residual isotope statistics. We extend the conventional time fit to an event-level muon-categorized joint time (J-MuCAT) fit, which uses the time to the most recent preceding muon in each energy-loss category. A complementary estimate is obtained from the candidate-to-muon-track distance distribution (TraDiTS). The muon-uncorrelated component is determined from the far-distance region and subtracted. This estimate is then used to constrain the J-MuCAT fit, defining the distance-constrained J-MuCAT (DCJ-MuCAT) fit. In detector-level simulation with successive cosmogenic-background vetoes, DCJ-MuCAT reduces the statistical uncertainty by more than $40\%$ relative to J-MuCAT and by more than $10\%$ relative to the TraDiTS. The fitted results remain consistent with the simulation truth. Applicability studies further show good performance over a broad range of muon rates and isotope fractions. The proposed framework provides a practical approach for estimating residual $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He backgrounds in large neutrino detectors.

</details>


### [111] [Extending the reconstruction of displaced tracks at CMS during the HL-LHC with Line Segment Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26695)
*Jade Chismar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS探测器为应对HL-LHC高堆叠事例环境而开发的Line Segment Tracking（LST）算法的升级版本，引入新型四联体（T4）结构，显著扩展了对长寿命粒子产生的位移轨迹的径向接受度（从40 cm提升至60 cm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC带来的极高堆叠（pileup）严重削弱传统跟踪算法对位移轨迹（displaced tracks）的重建效率与接受度，亟需高效、并行化且对位移敏感的新算法。

Method: 提出LST算法的增强实现——引入‘四联体’（quadruplet, T4）对象，即由共享一线段的两个triplet构成的链式结构；该结构专为识别大径向位移（R > 40 cm）的长寿命粒子衰变轨迹而设计，并在GPU上高效执行。

Result: T4增强型LST将位移轨迹的径向接受度从40 cm提升至60 cm，在多个物理场景中验证了其性能提升；相较无T4的LST，整体位移轨迹重建效率显著提高。

Conclusion: T4是LST算法的关键演进，使CMS在HL-LHC极端高堆叠条件下仍能稳健重建大位移轨迹，为B物理、奇异强子、暗物质探针等依赖长寿命粒子的物理分析提供了关键技术支持。

Abstract: The upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) to the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will greatly increase the average number of simultaneous proton-proton interactions per bunch-crossing (pileup), and will place significant demands on computing resources for the reconstruction of charged-particle tracks. Line Segment Tracking (LST) is a novel, highly parallelizable algorithm that can run efficiently on GPUs and has been integrated into the CMS software to enable the reconstruction of displaced tracks in the high pileup scenarios of the HL-LHC. The LST algorithm is designed to build track candidates using only outer tracker hits, naturally allowing for high acceptance of displaced track signatures. In the HL-LHC tracking reconstruction sequence without LST, displaced tracking ends at a radial displacement of the track origin of approximately 8 cm. The usage of LST in track reconstruction enhanced efficiency for displaced tracks, extending the acceptance in radial displacement from the track origin by more than a factor of 4. In this work, we introduce a new LST object, the quadruplet (T4), defined as a linked pair of triplets with a common line segment, targeting displaced track signatures. We present the performance of this improved implementation of the LST algorithm in several physics scenarios, extending the displaced-track acceptance from 40 cm to 60 cm in radial displacement compared to LST without T4s.

</details>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Gradient Flow for the Jet Transport Coefficient: A Quenched Lattice Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25089)
*Hai-Tao Shu,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用梯度流框架，在纯胶子（quenched）格点QCD中非微扰地计算了喷注输运系数 $\hat q$ 的介质贡献，发现 $\hat q/T^3$ 在 $T < T_c$ 严重压低，穿越去禁闭相变后迅速上升，并在 $0.76T_c \leq T \leq 2.25T_c$ 的去禁闭区近似恒定为 $\sim 1.1$，结果与先前不同重整化方案的格点计算一致，验证了梯度流方法对 $\hat q$ 的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定热QCD介质中的喷注淬火参数 $\hat q$ 对理解重离子碰撞中的能量损失机制至关重要；现有微扰计算在中等温度下不可靠，需非微扰格点方法；传统算符重整化存在系统误差，而梯度流提供了一套自洽、可推广至全QCD的算符定义与匹配框架。

Method: 采用梯度流定义的能量-动量张量 $T_{\mu\nu}^{\text{GF}}$ 提取熵密度 $s/T^3$，结合OPE中短距系数（一阶圈图微扰估计），重构 $\hat q/T^3$；使用三种格点间距进行连续极限外推；研究范围为纯胶子系综，聚焦渐近高能夸克的领头扭度（leading-twist）贡献。

Result: $\hat q/T^3$ 在 $T < T_c$ 区域显著抑制；在 $T \gtrsim T_c$ 后快速上升；在 $0.76T_c \leq T \leq 2.25T_c$ 的去禁闭相内稳定于 $\hat q/T^3 \simeq 1.1 \pm \text{stat.}$；该值与先前基于矩重整化（moment renormalization）的quenched格点结果在统计误差内一致。

Conclusion: 梯度流框架成功实现了 $\hat q$ 的非微扰格点计算，其系统性的算符重整化与匹配机制不仅适用于纯胶子理论，更可自然延拓至包含动力学夸克的全QCD——尤其关键的是，它能统一处理夸克流算符的重整化及其与胶子算符的混合问题。

Abstract: We apply the gradient-flow framework to determine the nonperturbative medium contribution to the jet transport coefficient $\hat q$ using quenched lattice QCD in the temperature range $0.76T_c \leq T \leq 2.25T_c$. The calculation focuses on the leading-twist contribution at leading order for an asymptotically energetic quark propagating through a thermal pure-gluon plasma. In the infinite-energy limit, the operator-product expansion expresses $\hat q/T^3$ in pure gauge theory as the product of a perturbative short-distance coefficient and the entropy density $s/T^3$. The latter is determined from the gradient-flow energy-momentum tensor at three lattice spacings for each temperature and the former is estimated perturbatively at one-loop order. We find that $\hat q/T^3$ is strongly suppressed below $T_c$, rises rapidly across the deconfinement transition, and remains approximately constant at $\hat q/T^3\simeq1.1$ throughout the deconfined temperature range studied. This is statistically consistent with the previous quenched lattice determination based on a different renormalization procedure, providing a nontrivial validation of the gradient-flow framework for the jet transport coefficient. The systematic treatment of operator renormalization and matching provided by gradient flow becomes particularly important in full QCD, because the need to renormalize quark operators and account for their mixing with the gluonic sector can likewise be addressed within the same framework.

</details>


### [113] [Experiment $\leftrightarrow$ lattice QCD: understanding high-temperature QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25191)
*Bedangadas Mohanty*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了相对论重离子碰撞实验与格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）在研究强相互作用物质热力学与相结构中的协同作用，重点涵盖状态方程、输运性质、守恒荷涨落、临界点搜寻，并强调了将平衡态格点计算与非平衡、有限尺寸的实验系统进行对接所面临的理论与方法学挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立QCD物质在高温高密下的相结构与热力学性质，需弥合实验观测（非平衡、有限系统）与第一性原理格点QCD计算（平衡态、连续极限）之间的鸿沟。

Method: 综述性分析，聚焦于格点QCD计算结果（如压强、熵密度、涨落、输运系数）与重离子碰撞可观测量（如集体流、粒子产额、净质子/净电荷高阶矩、能量扫描行为）之间的映射关系及系统误差评估。

Result: 明确了格点QCD对QCD状态方程、夸克胶子等离子体剪切粘滞比η/s、重子数涨落κσ²及临界点信号的定量约束；指出当前主要限制在于格点计算在有限μB下的符号问题、连续极限外推、以及实验中动力学演化与初始/末态效应的解耦困难。

Conclusion: 格点QCD与重离子碰撞实验的协同已进入定量检验阶段，未来在RHIC-BES II、LHC、FAIR、NICA及EIC上的多能区、多观测量联合分析，将推动QCD相图特别是临界区域的精确测绘。

Abstract: Relativistic heavy-ion collisions provide a unique experimental opportunity to study strongly interacting matter at extreme temperature and density, while lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) offers a first-principles approach to the equilibrium properties of such matter in the non-perturbative regime. The interplay between experiment and lattice QCD has therefore become central to establishing the properties and phase structure of QCD matter. Selected areas where this connection is particularly informative are discussed, including the QCD equation of state and its role in hydrodynamic descriptions of heavy-ion collisions, transport properties of the quark-gluon plasma, conserved-charge fluctuations and their relation to experimental cumulants, and the ongoing search for a critical point in the QCD phase diagram. Particular attention is given to the limitations involved in confronting equilibrium lattice calculations with the finite, dynamical and experimentally constrained systems produced in heavy-ion collisions. Recent developments increasingly allow quantitative tests of QCD thermodynamics over an extended range of temperature and baryon chemical potential. The continuing experimental programmes at RHIC and the LHC, together with future measurements at FAIR, NICA and the Electron-Ion Collider, provide important opportunities for an increasingly close interplay between lattice QCD, phenomenology and experiment.
